The greater surprise came at the window's edge in 89:17: failure to honor the unprotected orphan made `ف س د B001` readable as damaged distribution and support, not only spectacular imperial disorder.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2ffd509509642c940158","text":"The later love of wealth “جمًّا” showed that كثرة is not only imperial; the same multiplication principle reappears in ordinary human appetite.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001154/B001","root_001286/B001","root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4907ef6740e357db663f","text":"1. **Corruption as multiplied imbalance.** Lexical evidence: `ك ث ر B001` abundance and numerical increase; `ك ث ر B002` rivalry in abundance; `ف س د B001` corruption as departure from soundness and balance. Structural cue: the causative abundance follows transgression in the lands. Assigned role: `أَكْثَرُوا` makes فساد not a single act but a proliferating system. Abductive move: I infer that abundance here includes competitive magnification because later wealth-love (`ح ب ب B002`, `م و ل B001`, `ج م م B001`) gives a motive for multiplication. Reading change: the ayah reads as a regime that reproduces disorder.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60636e2aface7a9699f6","text":"2. **The many as dust before the lash-rain.** Lexical evidence: `ك ث ر B005` roiling abundant dust; `ص ب ب B001` later pouring from above; `س و ط B002` whip. Structural cue: increased فساد immediately triggers pouring punishment in 89:13. Assigned role: abundance becomes a raised, unsettled medium waiting to be driven down. Abductive move: I infer a material transition from roiling excess to poured discipline; the packet supplies abundance/dust and pouring. Reading change: `أكثروا` gains a physical texture of stirred-up excess, not only quantity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001154/B001","root_001286/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_88b640669b09fffa257c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:12:1\",\"root_001286\",[\"B005\"]],[\"89:12:3\",\"root_001154\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çoğalan bozukluk, havaya kalkıp ortalığı kaplayan yoğun bir toz gibi kabarır ve hemen ardından inecek karşılığa maddi bir zemin hazırlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_92772eeff65bb2b97a83","text":"2. **The inversion of supports into structural damage.** **Evidence/cue:** 89:7 supplies central supports through `ع م د B003/B007`, 89:8 supplies proportioned making through `خ ل ق B001/B003`, and the fixed `ف س د B001` negates repair, soundness, and balance. At the forward edge, 89:17 exposes failure to honor the orphan (`ك ر م B001`; `ي ت م B001`). **Mechanism:** structures that could sustain ordered life instead concentrate capacity until vulnerable members lose protection. The social endpoint is supplied, but the enabling arrow from concentrated support to deprivation is inferred. **Reading change:** *fasad* is a failure of the whole support-and-distribution system, visible in whom it leaves unsupported.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001154/B001","root_001286/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b5727d446ad6e8972239","text":"1. **Corruption scaled by competitive abundance.** **Evidence/cue:** `ك ث ر B001` is numerical increase and `ك ث ر B002` rivalry through greater wealth, number, or status; the backward context displays unmatched formation, rock works, and stakes. `ف س د B001` is departure from soundness and balance. **Mechanism:** monumental rivalry supplies productive capacity, while competitive increase directs that capacity into reproducing imbalance. The packet supplies scale and unsound outcome; I infer rivalry as the amplification mechanism. **Reading change:** the ayah describes industrial or institutional multiplication of corruption, not merely “many bad acts.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001154/B001","root_001286/B001","root_001286/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bcc191b451acf2b3cfa1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:12:1\",\"root_001286\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"89:12:3\",\"root_001154\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bozgunculuk, tek tek kötülüklerin toplamı değil, güç ve üstünlük yarışının durmadan çoğalttığı sistemli bir dengesizliktir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001154/B001","root_001286/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d5a50d6f591f9642d848","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:12:1\",\"root_001286\",[\"B001\"]],[\"89:12:3\",\"root_001154\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çoğaltılan bozulma, toplumun dayanak ve dağıtım düzenini işlemez hâle getirerek korunmasız kişileri desteksiz bırakır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
